2026-10-20T15:22:10.2483860Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Serve the 2-math-warpgroup Prefill MegaMoE with the per-warp FC1 epilogue on image 39379223361

Why: in kernel bench 39439372069 the best MegaMoE candidate so far was
fc1-2wg-host-epi. Gates passed and the output hashes match the control on
all 12 cases. Prefill MegaMoE times against the control (main's
sf128 + swizzle):

  balanced-1024 0.4806 -> 0.4553 ms (-5.3%)
  balanced-2048 0.8564 -> 0.8217 ms (-4.1%)
  balanced-4096 1.5724 -> 1.5157 ms (-3.6%)
  local-4096    1.4133 -> 1.3095 ms (-7.3%)
  skew-4096     1.7384 -> 1.6679 ms (-4.1%)

The candidate has two math warpgroups, each owning a 64x256 half of the
128x256 tile with a real m64n256 SS WGMMA. That needs the rebuilt DeepGEMM
host to launch 384 threads, which gives a 168-register cap. The FC1
epilogue runs per warp: branch-free SwiGLU, conflict-free staging, direct
16-byte global stores, and warp-level arrivals deferred to the next tile.
Offloading the FC2 scatter to the dispatch warps did not help: math then
waited on the FC2 stages, and the FC2 phase stayed NVLink-bound.

What: on top of the bench commits of this branch (image 39379223361 from
913f7bb, already pinned):
- configs/firmus-framework.json appends
  patches/deep-gemm-overlay/sm90-prefill-2wg-host-epi.patch to
  deep_gemm_overlay_patches. It is identical to the bench candidate's 03
  and is applied after sf128 and swizzle at provisioning.
- sglang.sbatch exports DG_SM90_PREFILL_2WG=1 for Prefill workers only and
  prints it on the worker start line. Decode keeps the image's 512/256-thread
  geometries, and its SASS is unchanged by the header patch.
SGLang overlays, EAGLE3 3/4, Decode cap 128 and concurrency 192 are as on
main.

Expected: Prefill MegaMoE is about 60% of a Prefill forward, so about -2%
on the Prefill step, i.e. +1-2% on the score against 8258.34. Precision is
unchanged: the outputs are byte-identical to the control in the kernel
bench.
